<header>
  <h1>Three tables, and the database only guards one of the links</h1>
  <p class="lede">The MySQL schema Hibernate builds from the entity classes. The solid line is a real foreign key. The dashed lines are plain id columns that the database does not check.</p>
</header>
<main>
  <section class="stage">
    <div class="scroll"><svg id="svg" viewBox="0 0 1200 800" role="img" aria-label="Entity relationship diagram of users, events and attendance"></svg></div>
    <div class="legend">
      <span><span class="pill pk">PK</span> primary key</span>
      <span><span class="pill fk">FK</span> enforced foreign key</span>
      <span><span class="pill lk">ref</span> reference with no constraint</span>
      <span>Hover a row to see which column it connects to.</span>
    </div>
  </section>

  <aside class="side">
    <div class="card">
      <h2>How the tables work together</h2>
      <ul>
        <li><b>users</b> holds admins and students in one table. The role column says which, and admin_code or roll_number is filled in for that role only.</li>
        <li><b>events</b> is created by an admin (created_by) and stores the geo-fence: latitude, longitude and radius_meters.</li>
        <li><b>attendance</b> is one row per successful scan, with the device and the measured distance.</li>
      </ul>
    </div>
  </aside>
</main>

<script>
(function(){
  var NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', RH=30, HEAD=52;
  var T = {
    events:{x:440,y:20,w:320,color:'#0B6E8A',sub:'JPA entity Event',cols:[
      ['id','BIGINT','PK'],['title','VARCHAR(255)  not null',''],['description','VARCHAR(255)',''],
      ['latitude','DOUBLE  not null',''],['longitude','DOUBLE  not null',''],['radius_meters','INT  default 50',''],
      ['event_date','DATE',''],['start_time','TIME',''],['end_time','TIME',''],['created_by','BIGINT','FK'],['created_at','DATETIME','']]},
    users:{x:10,y:450,w:320,color:'#12303A',sub:'JPA entity User / Admin / Student',cols:[
      ['id','BIGINT','PK'],['role','VARCHAR  ADMIN | STUDENT',''],['name','VARCHAR(255)  not null',''],
      ['email','VARCHAR(255)  unique',''],['password','VARCHAR(255)  not null',''],
      ['admin_code','VARCHAR  admin only',''],['roll_number','VARCHAR  student only',''],['created_at','DATETIME','']]},
    attendance:{x:870,y:450,w:320,color:'#5B4B8A',sub:'JPA entity Attendance',cols:[
      ['id','BIGINT','PK'],['event_id','BIGINT  not null','ref'],['student_id','BIGINT  not null','ref'],
      ['device_id','VARCHAR(255)  not null',''],['student_lat','DOUBLE',''],['student_long','DOUBLE',''],
      ['distance_meters','DOUBLE',''],['status','VARCHAR  default PRESENT',''],['timestamp','DATETIME  not null','']]}
  };
  Object.keys(T).forEach(function(k){ var t=T[k]; t.h=HEAD+t.cols.length*RH+6; });

  // relationships: from table/col -> to table/col
  var R = [
    {from:['events','created_by'], to:['users','id'],       logical:false, nFrom:'N', nTo:'1', label:'created by'},
    {from:['attendance','event_id'], to:['events','id'],    logical:true,  nFrom:'N', nTo:'1', label:'for event'},
    {from:['attendance','student_id'], to:['users','id'],   logical:true,  nFrom:'N', nTo:'1', label:'by student'}
  ];

  function colY(tn,cn){ var t=T[tn]; for(var i=0;i<t.cols.length;i++){ if(t.cols[i][0]===cn) return t.y+HEAD+i*RH+RH/2; } return t.y; }
  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');}

  var o='';
  // relationships first (under tables)
  R.forEach(function(r,i){
    var A=T[r.from[0]], B=T[r.to[0]];
    var ya=colY(r.from[0],r.from[1]), yb=colY(r.to[0],r.to[1]);
    var xa, xb;
    // choose facing sides
    if(A.x+A.w < B.x){ xa=A.x+A.w; xb=B.x; }
    else if(B.x+B.w < A.x){ xa=A.x; xb=B.x+B.w; }
    else { xa=A.x+A.w/2; xb=B.x+B.w/2; }
    // events -> users: events (top middle) to users (bottom left) overlap in x, so use side anchors
    if(r.from[0]==='events'){ xa=A.x; xb=B.x+B.w; }
    if(r.from[0]==='attendance' && r.to[0]==='events'){ xa=A.x; xb=B.x+B.w; }
    if(r.from[0]==='attendance' && r.to[0]==='users'){ xa=A.x; xb=B.x+B.w; }
    var mx=(xa+xb)/2, my=(ya+yb)/2;
    o+='<g class="rel'+(r.logical?' logical':'')+'" data-r="'+i+'">';
    o+='<path d="M'+xa+' '+ya+' C '+(xa+(xb-xa)*0.5)+' '+ya+', '+(xa+(xb-xa)*0.5)+' '+yb+', '+xb+' '+yb+'" fill="none" stroke="'+(r.logical?'#5B4B8A':'#12303A')+'" stroke-width="2" '+(r.logical?'stroke-dasharray="7 6"':'')+'/>';
    var sa=xa<xb?-1:1; // text offset direction
    o+='<text x="'+(xa+(xb>xa?14:-14))+'" y="'+(ya-8)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+r.nFrom+'</text>';
    o+='<text x="'+(xb+(xb>xa?-14:14))+'" y="'+(yb-8)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+r.nTo+'</text>';
    o+='<text class="lbl" x="'+mx+'" y="'+(my-8)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(r.label)+'</text>';
    o+='</g>';
  });

  Object.keys(T).forEach(function(k){
    var t=T[k];
    o+='<g class="tbl" data-t="'+k+'">';
    o+='<rect class="frame" x="'+t.x+'" y="'+t.y+'" width="'+t.w+'" height="'+t.h+'" rx="8"/>';
    o+='<path d="M'+t.x+' '+(t.y+HEAD)+' V'+(t.y+8)+' a8 8 0 0 1 8 -8 H'+(t.x+t.w-8)+' a8 8 0 0 1 8 8 V'+(t.y+HEAD)+' z" fill="'+t.color+'"/>';
    o+='<text class="tname" x="'+(t.x+16)+'" y="'+(t.y+23)+'">'+k+'</text>';
    o+='<text class="tsub" x="'+(t.x+16)+'" y="'+(t.y+41)+'">'+esc(t.sub)+'</text>';
    t.cols.forEach(function(c,i){
      var y=t.y+HEAD+i*RH;
      o+='<g class="row" data-t="'+k+'" data-c="'+c[0]+'">';
      o+='<rect class="rowbg" x="'+(t.x+2)+'" y="'+y+'" width="'+(t.w-4)+'" height="'+RH+'" fill="transparent"/>';
      if(i>0) o+='<line class="rowline" x1="'+t.x+'" y1="'+y+'" x2="'+(t.x+t.w)+'" y2="'+y+'"/>';
      if(c[2]){
        var col = c[2]==='PK' ? '#8A6A0B' : (c[2]==='FK' ? '#0B6E8A' : '#5B4B8A');
        o+='<rect x="'+(t.x+10)+'" y="'+(y+7)+'" width="28" height="16" rx="3" fill="'+col+'"/>';
        o+='<text class="bdg" x="'+(t.x+24)+'" y="'+(y+19)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+c[2]+'</text>';
      }
      o+='<text class="col" x="'+(t.x+46)+'" y="'+(y+20)+'">'+esc(c[0])+'</text>';
      o+='<text class="typ" x="'+(t.x+t.w-12)+'" y="'+(y+20)+'" text-anchor="end">'+esc(c[1])+'</text>';
      o+='</g>';
    });
    o+='</g>';
  });
  document.getElementById('svg').innerHTML=o;

  // hover: highlight related column rows
  var svg=document.getElementById('svg');
  function mark(tn,cn,on){
    svg.querySelectorAll('.row').forEach(function(r){
      if(r.dataset.t===tn && r.dataset.c===cn) r.querySelector('.rowbg').setAttribute('fill', on?'rgba(244,219,74,.6)':'transparent');
    });
  }
  svg.querySelectorAll('.row').forEach(function(r){
    var tn=r.dataset.t, cn=r.dataset.c;
    function go(on){
      mark(tn,cn,on);
      R.forEach(function(rel){
        if(rel.from[0]===tn && rel.from[1]===cn) mark(rel.to[0],rel.to[1],on);
        if(rel.to[0]===tn && rel.to[1]===cn) mark(rel.from[0],rel.from[1],on);
      });
    }
    r.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){go(true);});
    r.addEventListener('mouseleave',function(){go(false);});
  });

  document.getElementById('copy').addEventListener('click',function(){
    var txt=document.getElementById('sql').textContent, b=this;
    function done(){ b.textContent='Copied'; setTimeout(function(){b.textContent='Copy SQL';},1500); }
    if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){ navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(done,function(){}); }
    else { var ta=document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value=txt; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); try{document.execCommand('copy'); done();}catch(e){} document.body.removeChild(ta); }
  });
})();
</script>

<header>
  <h1>From QR scan to result, and where each failure exits</h1>
  <p class="lede">The scan workflow split by who does what. Each diamond is one check, and the first one that fails sends a specific error back to the student. Pick an outcome to light up its path.</p>

<main>
  <div class="tabs" id="tabs" aria-label="Outcome"></div>
  <section class="stage">
    <p class="verdict all" id="verdict"></p>

<script>
(function(){
  var NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  var LANES=[{n:'Student',x:10,w:190},{n:'Frontend (student.js)',x:200,w:240},{n:'Backend (Spring Boot)',x:440,w:460},{n:'Database (MySQL)',x:900,w:270}];

  // node: id, kind, cx, cy, w, h, text
  var N={
    start:{k:'start',x:110,y:80},
    scan:{k:'act',x:110,y:140,w:150,h:40,t:['Scan event QR']},
    gps:{k:'act',x:320,y:140,w:170,h:44,t:['Read GPS and','device ID']},
    post:{k:'act',x:320,y:215,w:170,h:40,t:['POST /api/scan']},
    call:{k:'act',x:740,y:215,w:190,h:40,t:['processAttendance()']},
    d1:{k:'dec',x:740,y:300,w:190,h:70,t:['Event exists?']},
    d2:{k:'dec',x:740,y:400,w:190,h:70,t:['Student already','marked?']},
    calc:{k:'act',x:740,y:490,w:190,h:40,t:['Haversine distance']},
    d3:{k:'dec',x:740,y:575,w:190,h:70,t:['Within radius?']},
    d4:{k:'dec',x:740,y:675,w:190,h:70,t:['Device used by','another student?']},
    save:{k:'act',x:740,y:770,w:190,h:40,t:['Save as PRESENT']},
    e1:{k:'err',x:540,y:300,w:150,h:40,t:['404 NOT_FOUND']},
    e2:{k:'err',x:540,y:400,w:150,h:44,t:['400 PROXY_ATTENDANCE','(already marked)']},
    e3:{k:'err',x:540,y:575,w:150,h:44,t:['400 LOCATION_MISMATCH']},
    e4:{k:'err',x:540,y:675,w:150,h:44,t:['400 PROXY_ATTENDANCE','(device reused)']},
    ok:{k:'good',x:540,y:770,w:150,h:40,t:['200 SUCCESS']},
    db1:{k:'db',x:1035,y:300,w:190,h:40,t:['SELECT event by id']},
    db2:{k:'db',x:1035,y:400,w:190,h:40,t:['SELECT by event + student']},
    db3:{k:'db',x:1035,y:675,w:190,h:40,t:['SELECT by event + device']},
    db4:{k:'db',x:1035,y:770,w:190,h:40,t:['INSERT attendance']},
    res:{k:'act',x:320,y:880,w:190,h:44,t:['Show result to student']},
    end:{k:'end',x:110,y:945}
  };
  function top(n){return n.k==='start'||n.k==='end'?[n.x,n.y-(n.k==='end'?11:9)]:[n.x,n.y-n.h/2];}
  function bot(n){return n.k==='start'||n.k==='end'?[n.x,n.y+(n.k==='end'?11:9)]:[n.x,n.y+n.h/2];}
  function lft(n){return [n.x-(n.w||18)/2,n.y];}
  function rgt(n){return [n.x+(n.w||18)/2,n.y];}
  function P(pts){return 'M'+pts.map(function(p){return p[0]+' '+p[1];}).join(' L');}

  // edges: from,to,pts,label,lx,ly,dashed
  var E=[
    ['start','scan',[bot(N.start),top(N.scan)]],
    ['scan','gps',[rgt(N.scan),lft(N.gps)]],
    ['gps','post',[bot(N.gps),top(N.post)]],
    ['post','call',[rgt(N.post),lft(N.call)]],
    ['call','d1',[bot(N.call),top(N.d1)]],
    ['d1','d2',[bot(N.d1),top(N.d2)],'yes',752,352],
    ['d1','e1',[lft(N.d1),rgt(N.e1)],'no',630,290],
    ['d2','e2',[lft(N.d2),rgt(N.e2)],'yes',630,390],
    ['d2','calc',[bot(N.d2),top(N.calc)],'no',752,452],
    ['calc','d3',[bot(N.calc),top(N.d3)]],
    ['d3','e3',[lft(N.d3),rgt(N.e3)],'no',630,565],
    ['d3','d4',[bot(N.d3),top(N.d4)],'yes',752,627],
    ['d4','e4',[lft(N.d4),rgt(N.e4)],'yes',630,665],
    ['d4','save',[bot(N.d4),top(N.save)],'no',752,727],
    ['save','ok',[lft(N.save),rgt(N.ok)]],
    ['e1','res',[lft(N.e1),[320,300],[320,top(N.res)[1]]]],
    ['e2','res',[lft(N.e2),[320,400],[320,top(N.res)[1]]]],
    ['e3','res',[lft(N.e3),[320,575],[320,top(N.res)[1]]]],
    ['e4','res',[lft(N.e4),[320,675],[320,top(N.res)[1]]]],
    ['ok','res',[lft(N.ok),[320,770],[320,top(N.res)[1]]]],
    ['res','end',[lft(N.res),[110,880],top(N.end)]],
    ['d1','db1',[rgt(N.d1),lft(N.db1)],'',0,0,true],
    ['d2','db2',[rgt(N.d2),lft(N.db2)],'',0,0,true],
    ['d4','db3',[rgt(N.d4),lft(N.db3)],'',0,0,true],
    ['save','db4',[rgt(N.save),lft(N.db4)],'',0,0,true]
  ];

  var COMMON=['start','scan','gps','post','call','d1','res','end'];
  var SC={
    all:{label:'All paths',cls:'all',text:'Every route through the scan. Pick an outcome to follow one of them.',set:null},
    ok:{label:'Attendance marked',cls:'ok',text:'All four checks pass and the row is saved as PRESENT (HTTP 200).',
        set:COMMON.concat(['d2','calc','d3','d4','save','ok','db1','db2','db3','db4'])},
    nf:{label:'Unknown QR code',cls:'err',text:'Stops at the first check: the event does not exist (HTTP 404, NOT_FOUND).',
        set:COMMON.concat(['e1','db1'])},
    dup:{label:'Already marked',cls:'err',text:'Stops at the second check: this student already has a row for the event (HTTP 400, PROXY_ATTENDANCE).',
        set:COMMON.concat(['d2','e2','db1','db2'])},
    far:{label:'Outside geo-fence',cls:'err',text:'Stops at the distance check: the student is farther than the event radius (HTTP 400, LOCATION_MISMATCH).',
        set:COMMON.concat(['d2','calc','d3','e3','db1','db2'])},
    proxy:{label:'Device reused',cls:'err',text:'Stops at the device check: the same phone already marked another student (HTTP 400, PROXY_ATTENDANCE).',
        set:COMMON.concat(['d2','calc','d3','d4','e4','db1','db2','db3'])}
  };

  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;');}
  var o='<defs><marker id="ar" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto"><path d="M1 1 L11 6 L1 11z" fill="#12303A"/></marker>'+
        '<marker id="ar2" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto"><path d="M1 1 L11 6 L1 11z" fill="#5B4B8A"/></marker></defs>';
  LANES.forEach(function(l,i){
    o+='<rect class="lane'+(i%2?' alt':'')+'" x="'+l.x+'" y="10" width="'+l.w+'" height="960"/>';
    o+='<text class="lname" x="'+(l.x+l.w/2)+'" y="34" text-anchor="middle">'+l.n+'</text>';
  });
  E.forEach(function(e,i){
    o+='<g class="edge'+(e[6]?' dashed':'')+'" data-f="'+e[0]+'" data-t="'+e[1]+'"><path d="'+P(e[2])+'" marker-end="url(#'+(e[6]?'ar2':'ar')+')"/>';
    if(e[3]) o+='<text x="'+e[4]+'" y="'+e[5]+'" text-anchor="middle">'+e[3]+'</text>';
    o+='</g>';
  });
  Object.keys(N).forEach(function(id){
    var n=N[id];
    o+='<g class="node '+(n.k==='dec'?'dec':n.k==='err'?'err':n.k==='good'?'good':n.k==='db'?'db':'')+(n.k==='start'?' dot':'')+'" data-id="'+id+'">';
    if(n.k==='start'){ o+='<circle class="shape" cx="'+n.x+'" cy="'+n.y+'" r="9"/>'; }
    else if(n.k==='end'){ o+='<circle cx="'+n.x+'" cy="'+n.y+'" r="11" fill="#fff" stroke="#12303A" stroke-width="1.7"/><circle class="shape" style="fill:#12303A" cx="'+n.x+'" cy="'+n.y+'" r="6"/>'; }
    else if(n.k==='dec'){
      o+='<polygon class="shape" points="'+n.x+','+(n.y-n.h/2)+' '+(n.x+n.w/2)+','+n.y+' '+n.x+','+(n.y+n.h/2)+' '+(n.x-n.w/2)+','+n.y+'"/>';
    } else {
      o+='<rect class="shape" x="'+(n.x-n.w/2)+'" y="'+(n.y-n.h/2)+'" width="'+n.w+'" height="'+n.h+'" rx="'+(n.k==='db'?4:14)+'"/>';
    }
    if(n.t){
      var ls=n.t, y0=n.y-(ls.length-1)*7+4;
      ls.forEach(function(s,i){ o+='<text x="'+n.x+'" y="'+(y0+i*15)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(s)+'</text>'; });
    }
    o+='</g>';
  });
  var svg=document.getElementById('svg'); svg.innerHTML=o;

  function show(key){
    var sc=SC[key], set=sc.set;
    svg.classList.toggle('fade', !!set);
    svg.querySelectorAll('.node').forEach(function(g){ g.classList.toggle('on', !set || set.indexOf(g.dataset.id)>-1); });
    var edges=Array.prototype.slice.call(svg.querySelectorAll('.edge'));
    edges.forEach(function(g){
      var on=!set || (set.indexOf(g.dataset.f)>-1 && set.indexOf(g.dataset.t)>-1);
      g.classList.toggle('on',on);
      if(on && set) svg.appendChild(g); // draw highlighted edges on top
    });
    // keep nodes above edges
    svg.querySelectorAll('.node').forEach(function(g){ svg.appendChild(g); });
    var v=document.getElementById('verdict'); v.className='verdict '+sc.cls; v.textContent=sc.text;
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function(t){ t.setAttribute('aria-pressed', t.dataset.k===key?'true':'false'); });
  }
  var tabs=document.getElementById('tabs');
  Object.keys(SC).forEach(function(k){
    var b=document.createElement('button'); b.className='tab '+SC[k].cls; b.dataset.k=k; b.textContent=SC[k].label;
    b.addEventListener('click',function(){show(k);}); tabs.appendChild(b);
  });
  show('ok');
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

  var NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');}
  function anchor(b,tx,ty){
    var cx=b.x+b.w/2, cy=b.y+b.h/2, dx=tx-cx, dy=ty-cy;
    if(!dx && !dy) return [cx,cy];
    var s=Math.min((b.w/2)/Math.max(Math.abs(dx),1e-9),(b.h/2)/Math.max(Math.abs(dy),1e-9));
    return [cx+dx*s, cy+dy*s];
  }

  function render(key){
    var v=VIEWS[key], B={}, maxX=0, maxY=0, out='';
    v.boxes.forEach(function(b){
      var lines=[].concat(b.attrs||[], b.meths||[]);
      var chars=lines.reduce(function(m,s){return Math.max(m,s.length);},0);
      b.w=Math.max(180, Math.round(Math.max(chars*7.6, b.name.length*9, (b.st||'').length*7)+34));
      var head=b.st?50:34, na=(b.attrs||[]).length, nm=(b.meths||[]).length;
      b.h=head+(na?na*LH+14:0)+(nm?nm*LH+14:0)+((!na&&!nm)?6:0);
      b.x=b.cx-b.w/2; B[b.id]=b;
      maxX=Math.max(maxX,b.x+b.w); maxY=Math.max(maxY,b.y+b.h);
    });
    var minX=Math.min.apply(null,v.boxes.map(function(b){return b.x;}));
    var shift = minX<16 ? 16-minX : 0;
    var W=maxX+shift+16, H=maxY+24;
    out+='<svg xmlns="'+NS+'" viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" id="svg" role="img" aria-label="UML class diagram: '+esc(v.label)+'" style="min-width:'+Math.min(W,1000)+'px"><g transform="translate('+shift+',0)">';
    out+='<defs>'+
      '<marker id="m-tri" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="11" refY="6" markerWidth="12" markerHeight="12" orient="auto"><path d="M1 1 L11 6 L1 11z" fill="#fff" stroke="#12303A" stroke-width="1.5"/></marker>'+
      '<marker id="m-open" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="11" refY="6" markerWidth="11" markerHeight="11" orient="auto"><path d="M2 1.5 L10.5 6 L2 10.5" fill="none" stroke="#12303A" stroke-width="1.7"/></marker></defs>';

    v.edges.forEach(function(e,i){
      var A=B[e.a], C=B[e.b];
      var p1=anchor(A,C.x+C.w/2,C.y+C.h/2), p2=anchor(C,A.x+A.w/2,A.y+A.h/2);
      var mk = (e.t==='inherit'||e.t==='realize') ? 'url(#m-tri)' : 'url(#m-open)';
      var dashed = (e.t==='realize'||e.t==='dep') ? ' dashed' : '';
      out+='<g class="edge'+dashed+'" data-a="'+e.a+'" data-b="'+e.b+'">';
      out+='<line x1="'+p1[0].toFixed(1)+'" y1="'+p1[1].toFixed(1)+'" x2="'+p2[0].toFixed(1)+'" y2="'+p2[1].toFixed(1)+'" marker-end="'+mk+'"/>';
      if(e.l) out+='<text x="'+((p1[0]+p2[0])/2).toFixed(1)+'" y="'+(((p1[1]+p2[1])/2)-6).toFixed(1)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(e.l)+'</text>';
      if(e.m1) out+='<text x="'+(p1[0]+(p2[0]-p1[0])*0.1).toFixed(1)+'" y="'+(p1[1]+(p2[1]-p1[1])*0.1-6).toFixed(1)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+e.m1+'</text>';
      if(e.m2) out+='<text x="'+(p1[0]+(p2[0]-p1[0])*0.9).toFixed(1)+'" y="'+(p1[1]+(p2[1]-p1[1])*0.9-6).toFixed(1)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+e.m2+'</text>';
      out+='</g>';
    });

    v.boxes.forEach(function(b){
      var head=b.st?50:34, y=b.y;
      out+='<g class="box" data-id="'+b.id+'" tabindex="0">';
      out+='<rect class="frame" x="'+b.x+'" y="'+b.y+'" width="'+b.w+'" height="'+b.h+'" rx="6"/>';
      out+='<path d="M'+b.x+' '+(b.y+head)+' V'+(b.y+6)+' a6 6 0 0 1 6 -6 H'+(b.x+b.w-6)+' a6 6 0 0 1 6 6 V'+(b.y+head)+' z" fill="'+TONE[b.tone]+'"/>';
      if(b.st){
        out+='<text class="st" x="'+(b.x+b.w/2)+'" y="'+(b.y+17)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(b.st)+'</text>';
        out+='<text class="name" x="'+(b.x+b.w/2)+'" y="'+(b.y+38)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(b.name)+'</text>';
      } else {
        out+='<text class="name" x="'+(b.x+b.w/2)+'" y="'+(b.y+23)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(b.name)+'</text>';
      }
      var cy=b.y+head;
      (b.attrs||[]).forEach(function(t,i){ out+='<text class="m" x="'+(b.x+12)+'" y="'+(cy+19+i*LH-2)+'">'+esc(t)+'</text>'; });
      if((b.attrs||[]).length){ cy+=b.attrs.length*LH+14; }
      if((b.attrs||[]).length && (b.meths||[]).length){ out+='<line class="sep" x1="'+b.x+'" y1="'+(cy-7)+'" x2="'+(b.x+b.w)+'" y2="'+(cy-7)+'"/>'; }
      (b.meths||[]).forEach(function(t,i){ out+='<text class="m" x="'+(b.x+12)+'" y="'+(cy+13+i*LH)+'">'+esc(t)+'</text>'; });
      out+='</g>';
    });
    out+='</g></svg>';

    var holder=document.querySelector('.scroll');
    holder.innerHTML=out;
    document.getElementById('note').textContent=v.note;
    var co=document.getElementById('callout');
    if(v.callout){ co.hidden=false; co.textContent=v.callout; } else { co.hidden=true; }

    var svg=holder.querySelector('svg');
    function hot(id){
      svg.classList.add('dim');
      svg.querySelectorAll('.box').forEach(function(x){ if(x.dataset.id===id) x.classList.add('hot'); });
      svg.querySelectorAll('.edge').forEach(function(x){
        if(x.dataset.a===id||x.dataset.b===id){
          x.classList.add('hot');
          svg.querySelectorAll('.box').forEach(function(bx){ if(bx.dataset.id===x.dataset.a||bx.dataset.id===x.dataset.b) bx.classList.add('hot'); });
        }
      });
    }
    function cool(){ svg.classList.remove('dim'); svg.querySelectorAll('.hot').forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove('hot');}); }
    svg.querySelectorAll('.box').forEach(function(x){
      x.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){hot(x.dataset.id);});
      x.addEventListener('mouseleave',cool);
      x.addEventListener('focus',function(){hot(x.dataset.id);});
      x.addEventListener('blur',cool);
    });
  }

  var tabs=document.getElementById('tabs');
  Object.keys(VIEWS).forEach(function(k,i){
    var b=document.createElement('button'); b.className='tab'; b.setAttribute('role','tab');
    b.setAttribute('aria-selected', i===0?'true':'false'); b.textContent=VIEWS[k].label;
    b.addEventListener('click',function(){
      tabs.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function(t){t.setAttribute('aria-selected','false');});
      b.setAttribute('aria-selected','true'); render(k);
    });
    tabs.appendChild(b);
  });
  render('domain');
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<header>
  <h1>One scan, five checks, five possible outcomes</h1>
  <p class="lede">When a student scans an event QR code, the backend validates it step by step and stops at the first check that fails. Pick an outcome to replay the exact path.</p>

<main>
  <aside class="rail" aria-label="Outcome">
    <h2>Outcome to replay</h2>
    <button class="opt" data-sc="ok"    data-kind="ok"  aria-pressed="true"><b>Attendance marked</b><span>200 · SUCCESS</span></button>
    <button class="opt" data-sc="far"   data-kind="err" aria-pressed="false"><b>Outside the geo-fence</b><span>400 · LOCATION_MISMATCH</span></button>
    <button class="opt" data-sc="proxy" data-kind="err" aria-pressed="false"><b>Device already used</b><span>400 · PROXY_ATTENDANCE</span></button>
    <button class="opt" data-sc="dup"   data-kind="err" aria-pressed="false"><b>Student already marked</b><span>400 · PROXY_ATTENDANCE</span></button>
    <button class="opt" data-sc="nf"    data-kind="err" aria-pressed="false"><b>Unknown QR code</b><span>404 · NOT_FOUND</span></button>
    <div class="order">
      The service runs its checks in this order:
      <ol>
        <li>Event exists</li>
        <li>Student has not marked yet</li>
        <li>Distance within radius (Haversine)</li>
        <li>Device not used by another student</li>
        <li>Save as PRESENT</li>

<script>
(function(){
  var LANES = [
    {id:'stu', name:'Student',                sub:'browser user'},
    {id:'ui',  name:'student.js',             sub:'scanner page'},
    {id:'ctl', name:'AttendanceController',   sub:'POST /api/scan'},
    {id:'svc', name:'AttendanceService',      sub:'processAttendance()'},
    {id:'repo',name:'Repositories',           sub:'Spring Data JPA · MySQL'},
    {id:'geo', name:'GeoUtils',               sub:'Haversine'},
    {id:'exh', name:'ExceptionHandler',        sub:'GlobalExceptionHandler'}
  ];
  var ALL=['ok','far','proxy','dup','nf'], PASS=['ok','far','proxy','dup'];

  // f=from, t=to, k=kind (call|ret|err|ok), s=scenarios, d=log text, c=code location
  var M = [
    {f:'stu',t:'ui', k:'call',l:'scan QR (eventId)',s:ALL,d:'The student scans the event QR code, which carries the eventId.',c:'student.js'},
    {f:'ui',t:'ui',  k:'call',l:'read GPS + deviceId',s:ALL,d:'The page reads GPS from the browser and a device ID saved in localStorage.',c:'utils.js getDeviceId()'},
    {f:'ui',t:'ctl', k:'call',l:'POST /api/scan',s:ALL,d:'Sends eventId, studentId, studentLat, studentLong and deviceId as JSON.',c:'student.js fetch()'},
    {f:'ctl',t:'svc',k:'call',l:'processAttendance(req)',s:ALL,d:'The controller hands the request to the service.',c:'AttendanceController'},
    {f:'svc',t:'repo',k:'call',l:'findById(eventId)',s:ALL,d:'Check 1: does this event exist?',c:'EventRepository'},

    {f:'repo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'no event found',s:['nf'],d:'No row came back for that eventId.',c:'EventRepository'},
    {f:'svc',t:'exh',k:'err',l:'throw ResourceNotFoundException',s:['nf'],d:'The service throws "Invalid QR code - Event not found".',c:'AttendanceService'},
    {f:'exh',t:'ui', k:'err',l:'404  NOT_FOUND',s:['nf'],d:'The handler wraps it as {status: ERROR, errorCode: NOT_FOUND}.',c:'GlobalExceptionHandler'},
    {f:'ui',t:'stu', k:'err',l:'"Invalid QR code"',s:['nf'],d:'The scanner page shows the error.',c:'student.js'},

    {f:'repo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'Event (lat, lon, radius)',s:PASS,d:'The event and its geo-fence (centre and radius) are loaded.',c:'Event entity'},
    {f:'svc',t:'repo',k:'call',l:'existsByEventIdAndStudentId',s:PASS,d:'Check 2: has this student already marked attendance for this event?',c:'AttendanceRepository'},
    {f:'repo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'true',s:['dup'],d:'Yes, a row already exists.',c:'AttendanceRepository'},
    {f:'svc',t:'exh',k:'err',l:'throw ProxyAttendanceException',s:['dup'],d:'The service throws "You have already marked attendance for this event".',c:'AttendanceService'},
    {f:'exh',t:'ui', k:'err',l:'400  PROXY_ATTENDANCE',s:['dup'],d:'Returned as a 400 with errorCode PROXY_ATTENDANCE.',c:'GlobalExceptionHandler'},
    {f:'ui',t:'stu', k:'err',l:'"Already marked"',s:['dup'],d:'The scanner page shows the error.',c:'student.js'},

    {f:'repo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'false',s:['ok','far','proxy'],d:'No earlier attendance by this student.',c:'AttendanceRepository'},
    {f:'svc',t:'geo',k:'call',l:'getDistance(event, student)',s:['ok','far','proxy'],d:'Check 3: Haversine distance between the event and the student, with Earth radius 6,371,000 m.',c:'util/GeoUtils.java'},
    {f:'geo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'distance in metres',s:['ok','far','proxy'],d:'Returns the distance in metres.',c:'GeoUtils'},

    {f:'svc',t:'exh',k:'err',l:'throw LocationMismatchException',s:['far'],d:'Distance is greater than radiusMeters, so the service throws "You are 500m away … Maximum allowed: 50m".',c:'AttendanceService'},
    {f:'exh',t:'ui', k:'err',l:'400  LOCATION_MISMATCH',s:['far'],d:'Returned as a 400 with errorCode LOCATION_MISMATCH.',c:'GlobalExceptionHandler'},
    {f:'ui',t:'stu', k:'err',l:'"You are 500 m away"',s:['far'],d:'The scanner page shows the distance and the allowed maximum.',c:'student.js'},

    {f:'svc',t:'repo',k:'call',l:'existsByEventIdAndDeviceId',s:['ok','proxy'],d:'Check 4: has this device already marked someone for this event?',c:'AttendanceRepository'},
    {f:'repo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'true',s:['proxy'],d:'Yes, the same device already marked another student.',c:'AttendanceRepository'},
    {f:'svc',t:'exh',k:'err',l:'throw ProxyAttendanceException',s:['proxy'],d:'The service throws "Proxy detected! This device has already marked attendance…".',c:'AttendanceService'},
    {f:'exh',t:'ui', k:'err',l:'400  PROXY_ATTENDANCE',s:['proxy'],d:'Returned as a 400 with errorCode PROXY_ATTENDANCE.',c:'GlobalExceptionHandler'},
    {f:'ui',t:'stu', k:'err',l:'"Proxy detected"',s:['proxy'],d:'The scanner page shows the error.',c:'student.js'},

    {f:'repo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'false',s:['ok'],d:'This device is new for the event.',c:'AttendanceRepository'},
    {f:'svc',t:'repo',k:'call',l:'save(Attendance PRESENT)',s:['ok'],d:'Check 5 passed: the attendance row is saved with status PRESENT and the measured distance.',c:'AttendanceRepository'},
    {f:'repo',t:'svc',k:'ret',l:'saved row (id)',s:['ok'],d:'MySQL returns the saved row.',c:'attendance table'},
    {f:'svc',t:'ctl',k:'ret',l:'Attendance',s:['ok'],d:'The service returns the saved Attendance.',c:'AttendanceService'},
    {f:'ctl',t:'ui', k:'ok', l:'200  SUCCESS',s:['ok'],d:'Wrapped as {status: SUCCESS, message: "Attendance marked successfully"}.',c:'ApiResponse.success()'},
    {f:'ui',t:'stu', k:'ok', l:'✔ attendance marked',s:['ok'],d:'The scanner page confirms and the admin dashboard count goes up.',c:'student.js'}
  ];

  var VERDICT = {
    ok:   ['ok','Attendance marked. All four checks passed and the row was saved as PRESENT.'],
    far:  ['err','Rejected at check 3. The student is outside the event radius, so nothing is saved.'],
    proxy:['err','Rejected at check 4. The same device was already used by another student, so nothing is saved.'],
    dup:  ['err','Rejected at check 2. This student has already marked attendance for this event.'],
    nf:   ['err','Rejected at check 1. The QR code points to an event that does not exist.']
  };

  var X0=80, DX=160, W=X0*2+DX*(LANES.length-1), Y0=128, DY=50, TOP=18, BOX_H=54;
  var xOf={}; LANES.forEach(function(l,i){ xOf[l.id]=X0+i*DX; });

  var svg=document.getElementById('svg'), logEl=document.getElementById('log'),
      statusEl=document.getElementById('status'), verdictEl=document.getElementById('verdict'),
      playBtn=document.getElementById('play');
  var current='ok', list=[], shown=0, timer=null, playing=true;
  var reduce=window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  var NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';

  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');}

  function build(){
    list = M.filter(function(m){return m.s.indexOf(current)>-1;});
    var H = Y0 + list.length*DY + 30;
    var out = '<svg xmlns="'+NS+'" viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'">';
    out += '<defs>';
    [['call','#0B6E8A'],['ret','#5E7079'],['err','#C2255C'],['ok','#1B8A57']].forEach(function(p){
      out += '<marker id="ah-'+p[0]+'" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="9" refY="5" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M0 0 L10 5 L0 10 z" fill="'+p[1]+'" stroke="none"/></marker>';
    });
    out += '</defs>';
    LANES.forEach(function(l){
      var x=xOf[l.id];
      out += '<line class="lifeline" x1="'+x+'" y1="'+(TOP+BOX_H)+'" x2="'+x+'" y2="'+(H-10)+'"/>';
      out += '<rect class="lane-box" x="'+(x-76)+'" y="'+TOP+'" width="152" height="'+BOX_H+'" rx="6"/>';
      out += '<text class="lane-name" x="'+x+'" y="'+(TOP+23)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(l.name)+'</text>';
      out += '<text class="lane-sub" x="'+x+'" y="'+(TOP+41)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(l.sub)+'</text>';
    });
    list.forEach(function(m,i){
      var y=Y0+i*DY, x1=xOf[m.f], x2=xOf[m.t];
      out += '<g class="msg k-'+m.k+'" data-i="'+i+'">';
      out += '<rect class="band" x="10" y="'+(y-22)+'" width="'+(W-20)+'" height="38" rx="4"/>';
      if(m.f===m.t){
        out += '<path class="self" d="M'+x1+' '+(y-8)+' h38 v22 h-38" marker-end="url(#ah-'+m.k+')"/>';
        out += '<text x="'+(x1+46)+'" y="'+(y+4)+'">'+esc(m.l)+'</text>';
      } else {
        var dir = x2>x1?1:-1;
        out += '<line x1="'+x1+'" y1="'+y+'" x2="'+(x2-dir*2)+'" y2="'+y+'" marker-end="url(#ah-'+m.k+')"/>';
        out += '<text x="'+((x1+x2)/2)+'" y="'+(y-8)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(m.l)+'</text>';
      }
      out += '<text class="n" x="18" y="'+(y+4)+'">'+(i+1)+'</text>';
      out += '</g>';
    });
    out += '</svg>';
    svg.outerHTML = out;
    svg = document.querySelector('.scroll svg');
    svg.id='svg';
    svg.setAttribute('role','img');
    svg.setAttribute('aria-label','Sequence diagram of the attendance scan');

    logEl.innerHTML = list.map(function(m,i){
      var a=LANES.filter(function(l){return l.id===m.f;})[0].name, b=LANES.filter(function(l){return l.id===m.t;})[0].name;
      return '<li data-i="'+i+'"><strong>'+(i+1)+'.</strong> '+esc(m.d)+' <code>'+esc(m.c)+'</code></li>';
    }).join('');
  }

  function paint(){
    var msgs=svg.querySelectorAll('.msg'), items=logEl.querySelectorAll('li');
    for(var i=0;i<msgs.length;i++){
      msgs[i].classList.toggle('on', i<shown);
      msgs[i].classList.toggle('now', i===shown-1 && shown<list.length);
      items[i].classList.toggle('seen', i<shown);
      items[i].classList.toggle('now', i===shown-1);
    }
    statusEl.textContent = 'step '+Math.min(shown,list.length)+' of '+list.length;
    var v=VERDICT[current];
    if(shown>=list.length){ verdictEl.className='verdict show '+v[0]; verdictEl.textContent=v[1]; }
    else { verdictEl.className='verdict'; }
    if(shown>=list.length) stop();
  }
  function tick(){ if(shown<list.length){ shown++; paint(); } }
  function start(){ stop(); if(reduce){ shown=list.length; paint(); return; } playing=true; playBtn.textContent='Pause'; timer=setInterval(tick,750); }
  function stop(){ if(timer){clearInterval(timer); timer=null;} playing=false; playBtn.textContent = shown>=list.length ? 'Done' : 'Play'; }
  function reset(){ shown=0; build(); paint(); start(); }

  document.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(function(b){
    b.addEventListener('click',function(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(function(o){o.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true'); current=b.getAttribute('data-sc'); reset();
    });
  });
  playBtn.addEventListener('click',function(){ if(shown>=list.length) return; if(playing) stop(); else start(); });
  document.getElementById('step').addEventListener('click',function(){ stop(); tick(); if(shown<list.length) playBtn.textContent='Play'; });
  document.getElementById('all').addEventListener('click',function(){ shown=list.length; paint(); });
  document.getElementById('replay').addEventListener('click',reset);

  reset();
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

  main{max-width:1400px; margin:0 auto; padding:18px clamp(16px,4vw,56px) 56px}

<body>
<header>
  <h1>Two actors, eight things they can do</h1>
  <p class="lede">What each role can do in GeoQR Attend. The green one is the heart of the project: marking attendance, which always checks location and device before saving. Choose a role to see only its features.</p>
</header>
<main>
  <div class="tabs" id="tabs"></div>
  <section class="stage">
    <div class="scroll"><svg id="svg" viewBox="0 0 1100 700" role="img" aria-label="Use case diagram with Student and Admin actors"></svg></div>
    <div class="legend">
      <span><i class="sw g"></i>core feature</span>
      <span><i class="sw y"></i>check that always runs as part of it</span>
      <span><i class="sw"></i>other feature</span>
      <span>Dashed arrows labelled «include» mean the check is always performed.</span>
    </div>
    <table>
      <thead><tr><th>Use case</th><th>Actor</th><th>What happens</th><th>Endpoint</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody>
        <tr><td>Log in</td><td>Both</td><td>Email, password and role are checked, then a session token is returned.</td><td><code>POST /api/auth/login</code></td></tr>
        <tr><td>Mark attendance</td><td>Student</td><td>Scans the QR code. The backend verifies the event, location and device before saving.</td><td><code>POST /api/scan</code></td></tr>
        <tr><td>View my attendance history</td><td>Student</td><td>Lists the student's own records, newest first.</td><td><code>GET /api/attendance/student/{id}</code></td></tr>
        <tr><td>Create event and QR code</td><td>Admin</td><td>Sets title, time, location and radius. A QR code for the event is generated.</td><td><code>POST /api/events</code></td></tr>
        <tr><td>View event attendance</td><td>Admin</td><td>Shows who attended an event and how many.</td><td><code>GET /api/attendance/event/{id}</code></td></tr>
        <tr><td>View dashboard</td><td>Admin</td><td>Recent scans and event list.</td><td><code>GET /api/attendance/recent</code></td></tr>
        <tr><td>View analytics</td><td>Admin</td><td>At-risk students under 75% and the 7-day trend.</td><td><code>GET /api/analytics/…</code></td></tr>
        <tr><td>Download report</td><td>Admin</td><td>Exports attendance as CSV or PDF.</td><td><code>GET /api/reports/csv</code></td></tr>
      </tbody>
    </table>
  </section>
</main>

<script>
(function(){
  var NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  var UC={
    login:{x:550,y:110,t:['Log in'],who:['s','a'],cls:''},
    mark:{x:400,y:250,t:['Mark attendance','by scanning QR'],who:['s'],cls:'core',rx:110,ry:40},
    geo:{x:225,y:400,t:['Verify location','(Haversine)'],who:['s'],cls:'sub'},
    dev:{x:400,y:430,t:['Detect proxy','device'],who:['s'],cls:'sub'},
    dup:{x:575,y:400,t:['Block duplicate','scan'],who:['s'],cls:'sub'},
    hist:{x:250,y:580,t:['View my attendance','history'],who:['s'],cls:''},
    create:{x:830,y:210,t:['Create event','and QR code'],who:['a'],cls:'',rx:100,ry:36},
    list:{x:830,y:330,t:['View event','attendance'],who:['a'],cls:''},
    dash:{x:830,y:440,t:['View dashboard'],who:['a'],cls:''},
    ana:{x:830,y:550,t:['View analytics','(at-risk, trend)'],who:['a'],cls:''},
    rep:{x:830,y:640,t:['Download report','(CSV / PDF)'],who:['a'],cls:'',rx:100,ry:30}
  };
  // actor anchors
  var ACT={s:{x:70,y:330,n:'Student'}, a:{x:1030,y:380,n:'Admin'}};
  var LINKS=[
    ['s','login'],['a','login'],['s','mark'],['s','hist'],
    ['a','create'],['a','list'],['a','dash'],['a','ana'],['a','rep']
  ];
  var INC=[['mark','geo'],['mark','dev'],['mark','dup']];

  function rx(u){return u.rx||90;} function ry(u){return u.ry||32;}
  function edge(ax,ay,u){ // point on ellipse toward (ax,ay)
    var dx=ax-u.x, dy=ay-u.y, a=rx(u), b=ry(u), k=1/Math.sqrt((dx*dx)/(a*a)+(dy*dy)/(b*b));
    return [u.x+dx*k, u.y+dy*k];
  }
  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;');}

  var o='<defs><marker id="ai" viewBox="0 0 12 12" refX="10" refY="6" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="10" orient="auto"><path d="M2 1.5 L10.5 6 L2 10.5" fill="none" stroke="#8A6A0B" stroke-width="1.8"/></marker></defs>';
  o+='<rect class="sys" x="170" y="20" width="760" height="665" rx="14"/>';
  o+='<text class="sysname" x="550" y="48" text-anchor="middle">GeoQR Attend system</text>';

  LINKS.forEach(function(l){
    var a=ACT[l[0]], u=UC[l[1]], p=edge(a.x,a.y-20,u);
    o+='<g class="link" data-who="'+l[0]+'" data-uc="'+l[1]+'"><line x1="'+a.x+'" y1="'+(a.y-20)+'" x2="'+p[0].toFixed(1)+'" y2="'+p[1].toFixed(1)+'"/></g>';
  });
  INC.forEach(function(l){
    var a=UC[l[0]], b=UC[l[1]], p1=edge(b.x,b.y,a), p2=edge(a.x,a.y,b);
    o+='<g class="link inc" data-who="s" data-uc="'+l[1]+'"><line x1="'+p1[0].toFixed(1)+'" y1="'+p1[1].toFixed(1)+'" x2="'+p2[0].toFixed(1)+'" y2="'+p2[1].toFixed(1)+'" marker-end="url(#ai)"/>'+
       '<text x="'+((p1[0]+p2[0])/2+(l[1]==='geo'?-34:l[1]==='dup'?34:18)).toFixed(1)+'" y="'+(((p1[1]+p2[1])/2)).toFixed(1)+'" text-anchor="middle">«include»</text></g>';
  });
  Object.keys(UC).forEach(function(id){
    var u=UC[id];
    o+='<g class="uc '+u.cls+'" data-id="'+id+'"><ellipse cx="'+u.x+'" cy="'+u.y+'" rx="'+rx(u)+'" ry="'+ry(u)+'"/>';
    var y0=u.y-(u.t.length-1)*8+4;
    u.t.forEach(function(s,i){ o+='<text x="'+u.x+'" y="'+(y0+i*16)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+esc(s)+'</text>'; });
    o+='</g>';
  });
  Object.keys(ACT).forEach(function(k){
    var a=ACT[k];
    o+='<g class="actor" data-who="'+k+'"><circle class="head fig" cx="'+a.x+'" cy="'+(a.y-48)+'" r="14"/>'+
       '<path class="fig" d="M'+a.x+' '+(a.y-34)+' V'+(a.y+14)+' M'+(a.x-26)+' '+(a.y-16)+' H'+(a.x+26)+' M'+a.x+' '+(a.y+14)+' L'+(a.x-20)+' '+(a.y+48)+' M'+a.x+' '+(a.y+14)+' L'+(a.x+20)+' '+(a.y+48)+'"/>'+
       '<text x="'+a.x+'" y="'+(a.y+72)+'" text-anchor="middle">'+a.n+'</text></g>';
  });
  var svg=document.getElementById('svg'); svg.innerHTML=o;

  var VIEWS={all:'Everyone',s:'Student',a:'Admin'};
  function show(k){
    svg.classList.toggle('fade',k!=='all');
    svg.querySelectorAll('.uc').forEach(function(g){
      var u=UC[g.dataset.id]; g.classList.toggle('on', k==='all' || u.who.indexOf(k)>-1);
    });
    svg.querySelectorAll('.link').forEach(function(g){
      g.classList.toggle('on', k==='all' || g.dataset.who===k);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(function(t){ t.setAttribute('aria-pressed', t.dataset.k===k?'true':'false'); });
  }
  var tabs=document.getElementById('tabs');
  Object.keys(VIEWS).forEach(function(k){
    var b=document.createElement('button'); b.className='tab '+(k==='all'?'x':k); b.dataset.k=k; b.textContent=VIEWS[k];
    b.addEventListener('click',function(){show(k);}); tabs.appendChild(b);
  });
  show('all');
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

/* ============================================
   GEOQR ATTENDANCE - ADMIN DASHBOARD STYLES
   ============================================ */

/* Navigation */

.navbar {
    min-height: 64px;
    padding: 0 var(--spacing-lg);
    background-color: var(--card-bg);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: var(--spacing-lg);
}

.nav-brand {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--spacing-sm);
    color: var(--primary);
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    font-weight: 700;
    white-space: nowrap;
}

.nav-links {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--spacing-sm);
    flex: 1;
    justify-content: center;
}

.nav-link {
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-md);
    color: var(--text-secondary);
    transition: var(--transition);
}

.nav-link:hover,
.nav-link.active {
    background-color: var(--bg);
    color: var(--primary);
}

.nav-user {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--spacing-md);
    white-space: nowrap;
}

/* Main Content */

.main-content {
    max-width: 1200px;
    margin: 0 auto;
    padding: var(--spacing-xl);
}

/* Tabs */

.tab-content {
    display: none;
}

.tab-content.active {
    display: block;
}

/* Statistics */

.stats-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    gap: var(--spacing-md);
}

.stat-card {
    background-color: var(--card-bg);
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
    padding: var(--spacing-lg);
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: var(--spacing-md);
}

===== FILE: frontend/js/admin.js =====
/* ============================================
   GEOQR ATTENDANCE - ADMIN DASHBOARD LOGIC
   ============================================ */

let currentUser = null;
let trendChart = null;


/* ============================================
   INITIALIZATION
   ============================================ */

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

    checkAuth();

    if (!currentUser) {
        return;
    }

    loadUserData();
    setupTabNavigation();
    setupEventForm();
    loadDashboardData();

    // Set today's date
    const eventDate = document.getElementById('eventDate');

    if (eventDate) {
        eventDate.valueAsDate = new Date();
    }

});


/* ============================================
   AUTHENTICATION CHECK
   ============================================ */

function checkAuth() {

    const user = localStorage.getItem(
        CONFIG.USER_KEY
    );

    if (!user) {

        window.location.href = 'index.html';

        return;
    }

    try {

        currentUser = JSON.parse(user);

    } catch (error) {

        console.error(
            'Invalid user data:',
            error
        );

        localStorage.removeItem(
            CONFIG.USER_KEY
        );

        window.location.href = 'index.html';

        return;
    }

    if (currentUser.role !== 'ADMIN') {

        window.location.href = 'student.html';

        return;
    }

}


/* ============================================
   LOAD USER DATA
   ============================================ */

function loadUserData() {

    const userName =
        document.getElementById('userName');

    if (userName && currentUser) {

        userName.textContent =
            currentUser.name;
    }

}


/* ============================================
   TAB NAVIGATION
   ============================================ */

function setupTabNavigation() {

    const navLinks =
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav-link');

    const tabContents =
        document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content');


    navLinks.forEach(link => {

        link.addEventListener('click', event => {

            event.preventDefault();

            const tabName =
                link.dataset.tab;


            // Update active navigation
            navLinks.forEach(item => {

                item.classList.remove('active');

            });

            link.classList.add('active');


            // Show selected tab
            tabContents.forEach(content => {

                content.classList.remove('active');

                if (
                    content.id ===
                    `${tabName}-tab`
                ) {

                    content.classList.add('active');
                }

            });


            // Load analytics when opened
            if (tabName === 'analytics') {

                loadAnalytics();
            }

        });

    });

}


/* ============================================
   EVENT CREATION
   ============================================ */

function setupEventForm() {

    const form =
        document.getElementById('eventForm');


    if (!form) {
        return;
    }


    form.addEventListener(
        'submit',
        async event => {

            event.preventDefault();


            const eventData = {

                title:
                    document.getElementById(
                        'eventTitle'
                    ).value,

                description:
                    document.getElementById(
                        'eventDescription'
                    ).value,

                eventDate:
                    document.getElementById(
                        'eventDate'
                    ).value,

                startTime:
                    document.getElementById(
                        'startTime'
                    ).value,

                endTime:
                    document.getElementById(
                        'endTime'
                    ).value,

                latitude:
                    parseFloat(
                        document.getElementById(
                            'latitude'
                        ).value
                    ),

                longitude:
                    parseFloat(
                        document.getElementById(
                            'longitude'
                        ).value
                    ),

                radiusMeters:
                    parseInt(
                        document.getElementById(
                            'radiusMeters'
                        ).value
                    ),

                createdBy:
                    currentUser.id

            };


            try {

                const response =
                    await fetch(
                        `${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/events`,
                        {
                            method: 'POST',

                            headers: {
                                'Content-Type':
                                    'application/json'
                            },

                            body:
                                JSON.stringify(
                                    eventData
                                )
                        }
                    );


                const result =
                    await response.json();


                if (
                    result.status ===
                    'SUCCESS'
                ) {

                    const eventId =
                        result.data.id;


                    // Generate QR
                    generateQRCode(eventId);


                    showMessage(
                        'Event created successfully!',
                        'success'
                    );


                    // Reset form
                    form.reset();


                    // Restore default values
                    document
                        .getElementById(
                            'eventDate'
                        )
                        .valueAsDate =
                        new Date();


                    document
                        .getElementById(
                            'latitude'
                        )
                        .value =
                        '12.9716';


                    document
                        .getElementById(
                            'longitude'
                        )
                        .value =
                        '77.5946';


                    document
                        .getElementById(
                            'radiusMeters'
                        )
                        .value =
                        '50';

                } else {

                    showMessage(
                        result.message ||
                        'Failed to create event',
                        'error'
                    );
                }


            } catch (error) {

                console.error(
                    'Error creating event:',
                    error
                );

                showMessage(
                    'Connection error. Please try again.',
                    'error'
                );

            }

        }
    );

}


/* ============================================
   QR CODE GENERATION
   ============================================ */

function generateQRCode(eventId) {

    const qrContainer =
        document.getElementById('qrcode');


    if (!qrContainer) {
        return;
    }


    // Clear previous QR
    qrContainer.innerHTML = '';


    /*
       Event ID is encoded as JSON
       as required by the project.
    */

    new QRCode(qrContainer, {

        text: JSON.stringify({
            eventId: eventId
        }),

        width: 256,

        height: 256,

        correctLevel:
            QRCode.CorrectLevel.H

    });

}


/* ============================================
   DASHBOARD DATA
   ============================================ */

async function loadDashboardData() {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/attendance/recent`
            );


        const result =
            await response.json();


        /*
           Backend handover uses the generic
           SUCCESS/ERROR wrapper.
        */

        if (
            result.status === 'SUCCESS'
        ) {

            updateRecentAttendance(
                result.data
            );

        } else if (
            Array.isArray(result)
        ) {

            // Also accept direct array response
            updateRecentAttendance(result);

        }


    } catch (error) {

        console.error(
            'Error loading dashboard data:',
            error
        );

    }

}


/* ============================================
   RECENT ATTENDANCE TABLE
   ============================================ */

function updateRecentAttendance(
    attendanceList
) {

    const tbody =
        document.getElementById(
            'recentAttendance'
        );


    if (!tbody) {
        return;
    }


    if (
        !attendanceList ||
        attendanceList.length === 0
    ) {

        tbody.innerHTML = `
            <tr>
                <td
                    colspan="4"
                    class="text-center text-muted"
                >
                    No attendance records yet.
                </td>
            </tr>
        `;

        return;
    }


    tbody.innerHTML =
        attendanceList
            .slice(0, 10)
            .map(record => {

                return `
                    <tr>

                        <td>
                            Student #${record.studentId}
                        </td>

                        <td>
                            Event #${record.eventId}
                        </td>

                        <td>
                            ${formatTime(
                                record.timestamp
                            )}
                        </td>

                        <td>
                            <span class="text-success">
                                ${record.status || 'SUCCESS'}
                            </span>
                        </td>

                    </tr>
                `;

            })
            .join('');

}


/* ============================================
   ANALYTICS
   ============================================ */

async function loadAnalytics() {

    try {

        // At-risk students
        const atRiskResponse =
            await fetch(
                `${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/analytics/at-risk`
            );


        const atRiskResult =
            await atRiskResponse.json();


        let atRiskStudents = [];


        if (
            Array.isArray(atRiskResult)
        ) {

            atRiskStudents =
                atRiskResult;

        } else if (
            atRiskResult.status ===
            'SUCCESS'
        ) {

            atRiskStudents =
                atRiskResult.data || [];

        }


        updateAtRiskTable(
            atRiskStudents
        );


        // Trend
        const trendResponse =
            await fetch(
                `${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/analytics/trend`
            );


        const trendResult =
            await trendResponse.json();


        let trendData = [];


        if (
            Array.isArray(trendResult)
        ) {

            trendData =
                trendResult;

        } else if (
            trendResult.status ===
            'SUCCESS'
        ) {

            trendData =
                trendResult.data || [];

        }


        updateTrendChart(trendData);


        // Calculate average attendance
        calculateAverageAttendance(
            atRiskStudents
        );


    } catch (error) {

        console.error(
            'Error loading analytics:',
            error
        );

        showMessage(
            'Unable to load analytics.',
            'error'
        );

    }

}


/* ============================================
   AT-RISK TABLE
   ============================================ */

function updateAtRiskTable(
    students
) {

    const tbody =
        document.querySelector(
            '#atRiskTable tbody'
        );


    const countElement =
        document.getElementById(
            'atRiskCount'
        );


    if (!tbody) {
        return;
    }


    if (countElement) {

        countElement.textContent =
            students.length;

    }


    if (
        !students ||
        students.length === 0
    ) {

        tbody.innerHTML = `
            <tr>
                <td
                    colspan="5"
                    class="text-center text-muted"
                >
                    No at-risk students.
                </td>
            </tr>
        `;

        return;
    }


    tbody.innerHTML =
        students
            .map(student => {

                const percentage =
                    Number(
                        student.attendancePercentage
                    ).toFixed(2);


                return `
                    <tr>

                        <td>
                            ${student.name}
                        </td>

                        <td>
                            ${student.rollNumber}
                        </td>

                        <td>
                            ${student.eventsAttended}
                        </td>

                        <td>
                            ${percentage}%
                        </td>

                        <td>
                            <span class="text-danger">
                                At Risk
                            </span>
                        </td>

                    </tr>
                `;

            })
            .join('');

}


/* ============================================
   TREND CHART
   ============================================ */

function updateTrendChart(
    trendData
) {

    const canvas =
        document.getElementById(
            'trendChart'
        );


    if (!canvas) {
        return;
    }


    const labels =
        trendData.map(
            item => item.date
        );


    const counts =
        trendData.map(
            item => item.count
        );


    const ctx =
        canvas.getContext('2d');


    // Destroy previous chart
    if (trendChart) {

        trendChart.destroy();
    }


    trendChart =
        new Chart(ctx, {

            type: 'line',

            data: {

                labels: labels,

                datasets: [{

                    label:
                        'Attendance',

                    data:
                        counts,

                    tension:
                        0.3,

                    fill:
                        false

                }]

            },

            options: {

                responsive: true,

                maintainAspectRatio:
                    true,

                scales: {

                    y: {

                        beginAtZero:
                            true

                    }

                }

            }

        });

}


/* ============================================
   AVERAGE ATTENDANCE
   ============================================ */

function calculateAverageAttendance(
    students
) {

    const element =
        document.getElementById(
            'avgAttendance'
        );


    if (!element) {
        return;
    }


    /*
       The handover gives the at-risk
       student percentage data.

       We display an average when
       percentage values are available.
    */

    if (
        !students ||
        students.length === 0
    ) {

        element.textContent =
            '0%';

        return;
    }


    const values =
        students
            .map(
                student =>
                    Number(
                        student.attendancePercentage
                    )
            )
            .filter(
                value =>
                    !Number.isNaN(value)
            );


    if (values.length === 0) {

        element.textContent =
            '0%';

        return;
    }


    const average =
        values.reduce(
            (sum, value) =>
                sum + value,
            0
        ) / values.length;


    element.textContent =
        `${average.toFixed(2)}%`;

}


/* ============================================
   REPORTS
   ============================================ */

async function downloadCSV() {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/reports/csv`
            );


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                'CSV download failed'
            );
        }


        const blob =
            await response.blob();


        downloadFile(
            blob,
            'attendance-report.csv'
        );


        showMessage(
            'CSV report downloaded.',
            'success'
        );


    } catch (error) {

        console.error(
            'CSV error:',
            error
        );

        showMessage(
            'Unable to download CSV.',
            'error'
        );

    }

}


async function downloadPDF() {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/reports/pdf`
            );


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                'PDF download failed'
            );
        }


        const blob =
            await response.blob();


        downloadFile(
            blob,
            'attendance-report.pdf'
        );


        showMessage(
            'PDF report downloaded.',
            'success'
        );


    } catch (error) {

        console.error(
            'PDF error:',
            error
        );

        showMessage(
            'Unable to download PDF.',
            'error'
        );

    }

}


/* ============================================
   FILE DOWNLOAD HELPER
   ============================================ */

function downloadFile(
    blob,
    filename
) {

    const url =
        window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);


    const link =
        document.createElement('a');


    link.href = url;

    link.download = filename;


    document.body.appendChild(link);

    link.click();

    document.body.removeChild(link);


    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

}

/* ============================================
   GEOQR ATTENDANCE - AUTHENTICATION
   ============================================ */

// Login form handler
//document
  //  .getElementById('loginForm')
    //.addEventListener('submit', handleLogin);
const loginForm = document.getElementById('loginForm');

if (loginForm) {
    loginForm.addEventListener('submit', handleLogin);
}

async function handleLogin(event) {

    event.preventDefault();

    const email = document.getElementById('email').value;
    const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
    const role = document.getElementById('role').value;

    const message = document.getElementById('loginMessage');

    message.textContent = 'Logging in...';
    message.style.color = 'var(--text-secondary)';

    try {

        const response = await fetch(
            `${CONFIG.API_BASE_URL}/auth/login`,
            {
                method: 'POST',

                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                },

                body: JSON.stringify({
                    email: email,
                    password: password,
                    role: role
                })
            }
        );

        const result = await response.json();

        if (result.status === 'SUCCESS') {

            // Store token
            localStorage.setItem(
                CONFIG.TOKEN_KEY,
                result.data.token
            );

            // Store user information
            localStorage.setItem(
                CONFIG.USER_KEY,
                JSON.stringify(result.data.user)
            );

            message.textContent = 'Login successful!';
            message.style.color = 'var(--success)';

            // Redirect according to role
            if (result.data.user.role === 'ADMIN') {

                window.location.href = 'admin.html';

            } else {

                window.location.href = 'student.html';

            }

        } else {

            message.textContent =
                result.message || 'Login failed.';

            message.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
        }

    } catch (error) {

        console.error('Login error:', error);

        message.textContent =
            'Unable to connect to the server.';

        message.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
    }
}


// Fill Admin demo credentials
function fillAdminCredentials() {

    document.getElementById('email').value =
        'admin@geoqr.com';

    document.getElementById('password').value =
        'admin123';

    document.getElementById('role').value =
        'ADMIN';
}


// Fill Student demo credentials
function fillStudentCredentials() {

    document.getElementById('email').value =
        'student@geoqr.com';

    document.getElementById('password').value =
        'student123';

    document.getElementById('role').value =
        'STUDENT';
}


// Logout
function logout() {

    localStorage.removeItem(CONFIG.TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(CONFIG.USER_KEY);

    window.location.href = 'index.html';
}

===== FILE: frontend/js/qr-generator.js =====
// ==========================================
// QR CODE GENERATOR
// ==========================================

function showQR(eventId) {

    // Get the QR modal
    const qrModal = document.getElementById('qrModal');

    // Get the place where QR will be generated
    const qrCode = document.getElementById('qrModalCode');

    // Check if elements exist
    if (!qrModal || !qrCode) {
        console.error("QR elements not found");
        return;
    }

    // Clear previous QR code
    qrCode.innerHTML = "";

    // Generate QR code
    new QRCode(qrCode, {
        text: eventId,
        width: 250,
        height: 250
    });

    // Show modal
    qrModal.style.display = 'flex';
}


// ==========================================
// CLOSE QR MODAL
// ==========================================

function closeQR() {

    const qrModal = document.getElementById('qrModal');

    if (qrModal) {
        qrModal.style.display = 'none';
    }
}


===== FILE: frontend/js/student.js =====
/* ============================================
   GEOQR ATTENDANCE - STUDENT DASHBOARD
   ============================================ */

let html5QrCode = null;
let scannerRunning = false;
let scanProcessed = false;

/* ============================================
   PAGE INITIALIZATION
   ============================================ */

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    loadStudentInfo();

    document
        .getElementById('startScannerBtn')
        .addEventListener('click', startScanner);

    document
        .getElementById('stopScannerBtn')
        .addEventListener('click', stopScanner);
});


/* ============================================
   LOAD STUDENT INFORMATION
   ============================================ */

function loadStudentInfo() {
    const user = getCurrentUser();

    if (!user) {
        window.location.href = 'index.html';
        return;
    }

    document.getElementById('studentName').textContent =
        user.name || 'Student';

    document.getElementById('studentId').textContent =
        user.id || '-';

    document.getElementById('deviceId').textContent =
        getDeviceId();
}


/* ============================================
   START QR SCANNER
   ============================================ */

async function startScanner() {
    if (scannerRunning) {
        return;
    }

    scanProcessed = false;

    const status = document.getElementById('scannerStatus');
    const startButton = document.getElementById('startScannerBtn');
    const stopButton = document.getElementById('stopScannerBtn');

    status.textContent = 'Starting camera...';

    try {
        html5QrCode = new Html5Qrcode('reader');

        await html5QrCode.start(
            {
                facingMode: 'environment'
            },
            {
                fps: 10,
                qrbox: {
                    width: 250,
                    height: 250
                },
                aspectRatio: 1.0
            },
            onScanSuccess,
            onScanFailure
        );

        scannerRunning = true;

        startButton.disabled = true;
        stopButton.disabled = false;

        status.textContent = 'Scanner is active. Point your camera at the event QR code.';

    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Scanner error:', error);

        status.textContent =
            'Unable to start camera. Please allow camera permission.';

        showMessage(
            'Camera permission is required to scan the QR code.',
            'error'
        );
    }
}


/* ============================================
   QR SCAN SUCCESS
   ============================================ */

async function onScanSuccess(decodedText) {

    if (scanProcessed) {
        return;
    }

    scanProcessed = true;

    console.log('QR Code detected:', decodedText);

    try {
        const qrData = JSON.parse(decodedText);

        if (!qrData.eventId) {
            throw new Error('Invalid QR code');
        }

        await stopScanner();

        document.getElementById('scannerStatus').textContent =
            'QR detected. Getting your location...';

        await submitAttendance(qrData.eventId);

    } catch (error) {

        console.error('QR parsing error:', error);

        scanProcessed = false;

        showScanResult(
            'Invalid QR code. Please scan a valid GeoQR event code.',
            'error'
        );

        showMessage(
            'Invalid QR code.',
            'error'
        );
    }
}


/* ============================================
   QR SCAN FAILURE
   ============================================ */

function onScanFailure(errorMessage) {
    // Continuous scanning produces many temporary
    // "QR code not found" messages. We intentionally
    // do not display them to the user.
}


/* ============================================
   GET CURRENT LOCATION
   ============================================ */

function getCurrentLocation() {

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {

        if (!navigator.geolocation) {
            reject(
                new Error('Geolocation is not supported by this browser.')
            );
            return;
        }

        navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
            position => {

                let latitude = position.coords.latitude;
                let longitude = position.coords.longitude;

                /*
                 * Demo requirement from the project PDF:
                 * Simulate Location (500m away)
                 */
                const simulateLocation =
                    document.getElementById('simulateLocation').checked;

                if (simulateLocation) {
                    latitude += 0.0045;
                }

                resolve({
                    latitude: latitude,
                    longitude: longitude
                });
            },

            error => {

                console.error('Location error:', error);

                reject(
                    new Error(
                        'Unable to get your location. Please allow location access.'
                    )
                );
            },

            {
                enableHighAccuracy: true,
                timeout: 10000,
                maximumAge: 0
            }
        );
    });
}


/* ============================================
   SUBMIT ATTENDANCE
   ============================================ */

async function submitAttendance(eventId) {

    try {

        document.getElementById('scannerStatus').textContent =
            'Getting your location...';

        const location = await getCurrentLocation();

        const user = getCurrentUser();

        if (!user || !user.id) {
            throw new Error('Student information not found.');
        }

        const payload = {
            eventId: Number(eventId),
            studentId: Number(user.id),
            studentLat: location.latitude,
            studentLong: location.longitude,
            deviceId: getDeviceId()
        };

        console.log('Attendance payload:', payload);

        document.getElementById('scannerStatus').textContent =
            'Submitting attendance...';

        const response = await fetch(
            CONFIG.API_BASE_URL + '/scan',
            {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify(payload)
            }
        );

        const data = await response.json();

        console.log('Attendance response:', data);

        if (response.ok && data.status === 'SUCCESS') {

            showScanResult(
                data.message || 'Attendance marked successfully',
                'success'
            );

            showMessage(
                'Attendance marked successfully!',
                'success'
            );

            document.getElementById('scannerStatus').textContent =
                'Attendance submitted successfully.';

        } else {

            handleScanError(data);
        }

    } catch (error) {

        console.error('Attendance submission error:', error);

        showScanResult(
            error.message || 'Something went wrong. Please try again.',
            'error'
        );

        showMessage(
            error.message || 'Unable to mark attendance.',
            'error'
        );

        document.getElementById('scannerStatus').textContent =
            'Attendance submission failed.';
    }
}


/* ============================================
   HANDLE BACKEND SCAN ERRORS
   ============================================ */

function handleScanError(data) {

    let message = data.message || 'Attendance could not be marked.';

    switch (data.errorCode) {

        case 'LOCATION_MISMATCH':
            message = data.message ||
                'You are outside the allowed attendance location.';
            break;

        case 'PROXY_ATTENDANCE':
            message = data.message ||
                'Proxy attendance detected.';
            break;

        case 'NOT_FOUND':
            message = data.message ||
                'Invalid QR code - Event not found.';
            break;

        default:
            break;
    }

    showScanResult(message, 'error');

    showMessage(message, 'error');

    document.getElementById('scannerStatus').textContent =
        'Attendance could not be marked.';
}


/* ============================================
   STOP QR SCANNER
   ============================================ */

async function stopScanner() {

    if (!html5QrCode || !scannerRunning) {
        return;
    }

    try {

        await html5QrCode.stop();

        html5QrCode.clear();

    } catch (error) {

        console.error('Error stopping scanner:', error);

    } finally {

        scannerRunning = false;

        document.getElementById('startScannerBtn').disabled = false;
        document.getElementById('stopScannerBtn').disabled = true;

        document.getElementById('scannerStatus').textContent =
            'Scanner stopped.';
    }
}


/* ============================================
   DISPLAY SCAN RESULT
   ============================================ */

function showScanResult(message, type) {

    const result = document.getElementById('scanResult');

    result.textContent = message;
    result.className = `scan-result ${type}`;
}


===== FILE: frontend/js/utils.js =====
/* ============================================
   GEOQR ATTENDANCE - UTILITY FUNCTIONS
   ============================================ */

function getDeviceId() {

    let deviceId = localStorage.getItem(
        CONFIG.DEVICE_ID_KEY
    );

    if (!deviceId) {

        deviceId =
            'dev-' + generateRandomString(12);

        localStorage.setItem(
            CONFIG.DEVICE_ID_KEY,
            deviceId
        );
    }

    return deviceId;
}


function generateRandomString(length) {

    const chars =
        'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789';

    let result = '';

    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {

        result += chars.charAt(
            Math.floor(
                Math.random() * chars.length
            )
        );
    }

    return result;
}


function formatTime(timestamp) {

    const date = new Date(timestamp);

    return date.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit'
    });
}


function formatDate(dateString) {

    const date = new Date(dateString);

    return date.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
        month: 'short',
        day: 'numeric'
    });
}


function showMessage(
    message,
    type = 'info'
) {

    const toast =
        document.createElement('div');

    toast.className =
        `toast toast-${type}`;

    toast.textContent = message;

    document.body.appendChild(toast);

    setTimeout(
        () => toast.classList.add('show'),
        100
    );

    setTimeout(() => {

        toast.classList.remove('show');

        setTimeout(
            () => toast.remove(),
            300
        );

    }, 3000);
}


function formatDistance(meters) {

    if (meters < 1000) {

        return `${Math.round(meters)}m`;

    }

    return `${(meters / 1000).toFixed(1)}km`;
}
